## Analyzer Architecture Summary

llama-stack is a Kubernetes component. Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 8501. connecting to external services\: aiohttp, chromadb, httpx, llama-stack, milvus, mongodb, openai, postgres, qdrant, redis, requests, sqlalchemy, sqlite. runtime dependencies\: Redis. 69 Python package dependencies (69 required), categories\: 1 data, 1 image-processing, 1 llm-sdk, 1 numerical, 1 serialization, 1 tokenizer, 3 web-framework. Python k8s client with 120 API call(s). references components\: model-registry (import), vllm (import, provider).
